===== REQUIREMENT =====
# req-v1 — hotfix-channel：change 外的最小回写单元

target lineage: on-the-fly 分支（v4 产品线；main 暂不动，owner 指示；不合并 v1/v3）。state A 含已归档的 P0 前三 change（id-pattern 配置、change-scoped verify、modified-integrity 报告，KB source-commit 4127653）。

## 背景（墙二 + 样本四实证，复盘为据）

- **紧急修复全部逃逸流程**：v5.1 落地 UAT 四单（1012767/1012739/1012772/1012769）小时级响应压力下全走"会话直修→合并推送"，无一开 change。后果实测：MACHINE_CONFLICT 分支从代码删除而 store 的 BR-007 仍写旧口径（**已知 spec 漂移**）；1012769——全部 14 单里口径含量最高的一单——**档案目录里没有任何 .md**（只有截图和排查 SQL），根因链/口径变更决策/影响面只存在于会话记忆与 commit message。
- **留痕质量精确反映紧急度而非重要度**：同批 013 有完整 analysis.md（走了方案先行），014 什么都没有（边查边修）。
- **"不用修"的排查无承接**：UAT 轮 3/4 单实为环境/数据/基准问题——只排查不改码的工作今天连一个归档的地方都没有；且测试报单准确率随轮次递减（75%→25%），越到后期这类工作占比越高。
- **上线后学到的业务事实无落点**：「换机会在新后台重建运维机器并迁移主板」这类血泪知识，除非再开一个 change，否则 truth 的 Decisions 节无从追加。
- **成本刻度（复盘原话）**："流程外快在省掉了评审与留痕，而不是快在做得更聪明"；hotfix 的设计目标是把两者解耦——**留痕的最小成本必须压到比"不记录"只贵几分钟**，否则照样被逃逸。

## 目标（目标态 B）——设计先行：本节陈述目标与约束，B2/B5 的具体机制是 gate③ 必停的设计决策

### B1. 新概念：hotfix = change 外的最小回写单元，承接三类对象

1. **紧急代码修复的事后补记**：结论一段 + spec delta（沿用现行 delta 格式与 CAS）+ 测试绑定（或显式 no-test 理由）。
2. **"不用修"的排查结论**：结论一段即全部（现象→排查路径→定性→处置），无 delta。
3. **上线后学到的业务事实**：KB Decisions 节的 change 外追加条目（可与 1/2 并存于同一 bundle）。

### B2. CLI 面（形态候选，设计裁量——gate③ 决）

- 脚手架：`apriori hotfix <name>`（或 `apriori new --hotfix <name>`）——生成最小 bundle；**一条命令归档**：merge delta（若有）+ Decisions 追加（若有）+ 原子移入 archive（复用现行 archive 机械：CAS、原子提交、modified-integrity 报告白捡）。
- bundle 位置候选：a) `apriori/changes/<name>/` 加 `type: hotfix` 标记（复用 resolve/status/gate 生态）；b) 独立命名空间。倾向 a（少一套路径规则），评审与人类裁。
- bundle 内容（最小集）：单文件状态头（change/type/date/结论），可选 `specs/` delta，可选 decisions 追加段，delta 存在且无绑定测试时**必须**有机器可见的 no-test 理由行。

### B3. 与既有机械的关系

- 归档后的 hotfix 场景与普通场景**同权重**：verify/gate/check 一视同仁（这正是回写的意义——漂移终结于 store）。
- `status` 列出未归档 hotfix；`verify --change` 的兄弟归因把活 hotfix 的 delta 当兄弟对待（现行为自动成立，需测试确认）。
- CAS 照常强制（紧急不豁免正确性护栏——它只花 5 秒）。

### B4. RUNBOOK 语义（EN/CN）

新增 hotfix channel 节：何时合法（三类承接对象；事后补记为主形态——修复已经发生，通道的职责是让回写便宜）、何时禁止（常规演化必须走 change——防逃逸判据见 Q5）、与 STEP0-6 的关系（hotfix 不是流程的第七步，是流程边界外的最小合规路径）、人类角色（见 Q1）。runbook-version 是否动：Q4。

### B5. 纪律边界（别改清单交互——显式让渡与补偿，gate③ 必审）

hotfix **有意没有**：R2 评审轮、issue ledger、STEP0-5。补偿机制：
- bundle 全存档（结论是强制的——1012769 型空白档案不再可能）；
- **人类在场假设**：hotfix 场景里人类本来就在驱动（小时级响应），通道假定并要求这一点——具体机制（调用即批准 vs 显式 sign-off 标记）= Q1，涉及 gate④（KB 签收）不可合并原则的映射，**必停人类裁**；
- 后续任何 change 的评审天然看得见 hotfix 归档物（store 与 truth 里的痕迹同权重）；
- 别改清单五项（R2、flow-state verbatim、三态、账本、raw 强制）在**正式 change 流程内**分毫不动——hotfix 不修改它们，只是在流程外新增一条最薄路径。

## 范围外（won't do）

- 不改 STEP0-6 任何一步的语义；不改三个不可合并关卡；不做"hotfix 自动升格为 change"的自动化（人判断）；不做通知/推送类功能；不做对旧漂移的追溯回填工具（人工用本通道逐个补即可）。

## 验收标准（可测；细化待 STEP2）

- AC1（成本刻度）：lab 实测——三类承接对象各走一遍完整 hotfix（scaffold→填写→归档），每类的命令数与人工输入量记录在案，全流程 ≤10 分钟量级（以步骤数与文件数为客观代理）。
- AC2（漂移终结）：BR-007 型场景重演——代码删除某分支后，hotfix 携 REMOVED delta 归档 → `verify --specs` 不再要求该场景、其 lingering 测试转 ORPHAN；modified-integrity/CAS 全程生效。
- AC3（no-test 理由）：含 delta 无测试绑定的 hotfix，归档物中 no-test 理由机器可见；缺理由则归档拒绝（fail-closed）。
- AC4（纯结论）：无 delta 的 hotfix 归档只移 bundle，store/truth 零变化。
- AC5（Decisions 追加）：hotfix 携 decisions 条目归档 → truth/<module>.md Decisions 节追加该条（格式与现行一致，含 active 状态与出处）；Contract 节与 source-commit 不动。
- AC6（生态兼容）：status 列出活 hotfix；兄弟归因对活 hotfix delta 生效；归档后 gate/check/verify 全绿路径存在；存量 318 测试保持绿。
- AC7（文档）：RUNBOOK 双语新节 + docs/cli 双语 + CHANGELOG；check --self 绿。
- AC8（真实材料反事实）：用样本（副本）里 1012769 的真实材料（截图内容摘要 + 排查 SQL 结论）演示补记一个 hotfix bundle——当年的空白档案在本通道下的应然形态，存 review/ 作证据。

## 开放问题（设计先行——gate②/③ 人类必答）

- **Q1（核心）**：gate④（KB 签收）不可合并原则如何映射到 hotfix？候选：a) 调用即批准——hotfix 命令由人类亲手跑，命令行为即签收（bundle 状态头记录）；b) 归档命令要求显式 `--signed-off-by <人>` 类标记；c) 无 delta/decisions 的纯结论 hotfix 免签、有回写的必签。倾向 c 的分级，但这是三不可合并关卡的边界解释，**必须你裁**。
- **Q2**：bundle 位置（changes/ 复用 + type 标记 vs 独立目录）。倾向复用。
- **Q3**：防逃逸判据——什么时候"这不该是 hotfix，去开 change"？候选：mutation 操作数上限（如 >N 个 MODIFIED/REMOVED 即拒绝）、模块数上限、还是仅 RUNBOOK 文字约束 + 归档物天然可审计。倾向软约束（文字）+ 一个 advisory 提示，硬上限易被真实紧急场景反噬。
- **Q4**：runbook-version 4.0 → 4.1？（新增流程语义节但不改既有步骤。）
- **Q5**：命名——`hotfix` 还是更广（它也承接排查结论与知识回流，`回写单元`性质大于`修复`）？复盘用词 hotfix，沿用之，除非你想改。

## 四、棕地撞出来的四堵墙（本文核心增量）

### 墙一：store 是"累积的"，而工具的默认视角是"全量的"

单 change 视角下 verify/gate 的设计自洽；但棕地 store 会累积：
历史 change 的场景没有自动化测试（human 联调替代）、带字母后缀的 ID、
8 个 change 并行未归档。于是：

- `gate` C1 用默认 id-pattern 对全 store 跑 → **恒 BLOCKED**（`lib/gate.js` 的 flags
  没有 `--id-pattern`，而 `lib/spec-runner.js` 有——一个参数的差距让 gate 失去信号价值，
  "永远红的告警等于没有告警"，连带 C2~C7 的信用一起透支）
- `verify --change` 与 `--specs` 在多 change 并行下**都不干净**（前者被历史 UNBOUND 淹没、
  后者把别人的测试全判 ORPHAN），"本 change 是否达标"要跑两个命令再人工写一段解释，
  每个 change 重写一遍

**机制层的根因**：工具把"store 的健康"与"本 change 的达标"混在一个视角里。
棕地需要这两个问题分开回答，且都有一等公民的命令。

### 墙二：spec 只在 change 内演化，但现实在 change 外演化

两个独立事件指向同一个洞：

1. **UAT 四单紧急修复全部逃逸流程**——小时级响应压力下没人会开 change，
   于是 MACHINE_CONFLICT 分支从代码里删了，spec store 的 BR-007 还写着旧口径。
   **这不是纪律问题，是流程没有提供匹配紧急度的通道**。
   最扎眼的物证：通读缺陷档案时发现 **1012769 的档案目录里没有任何 .md**——
   只有两张截图和一份排查 SQL。这个单的完整根因链（换机→主板迁移→假冲突→丢单）、
   口径变更决策、UAT 实测影响面（3 单/133 元），全部只存在于对话记录和 commit message 里。
   同一批的 013 有完整 analysis.md（因为它走了"方案先行"），014 什么都没有
   （因为它是边查边修的）——**留痕质量精确反映了紧急度，而不是重要度**。
   1012769 恰恰是全部 14 单里口径含量最高的一单。
2. **1012769 的知识盲区**——换机业务链是评审双方共同不知道的，上线后数据回流才补上。
   补上之后呢？现状没有任何结构承接"上线后学到的业务事实"，除非再开一个 change。

**机制层的根因**：apriori 目前是"change 驱动"的——一切演化发生在 change 生命周期内。
棕地的现实是**演化也发生在 change 之间**：紧急修复、上线回流。
缺一个"change 外的最小回写单元"。

### 墙三：真实数据的接触时机太晚，且没有结构承接接触结果

前两轮 13 个缺陷里，**7 个是码值/字典/空列类**——全部属于
"代码的假设 vs 库里的现实"。既有文档已给出假设登记方案（层次抽对了：
登记的是假设，不是数据；DDL 注释是设计意图不是事实）。本文从棕地视角补一刀：

- **绿地没有这个问题**——没有十年沉积的数据可以背叛你。这类缺陷是棕地专属，
  而 runbook 的 STEP1 explore 对"数据现实"只字未提，它的产出（gap report）
  是面向代码结构的。
- UAT 轮又验证了一次：1012772 的悬空指针、1012769 的换机迁移，都是
  "数据的实际演化方式"超出了"表结构声明的语义"。**棕地的 explore 必须
  把数据当成与代码平级的勘探对象。**

===== SOURCE §五 样本四 =====
### 样本四：流程外的对照组（UAT 四单紧急修复）

- 单均耗时**小时级**（最快的 1012767 从定性到三线推送约 1.5 小时），
  看起来远快于流程内——但要算上隐性成本：
  1012769 的排查跨了**多轮对话、数次人肉 SQL 中转**（没有 UAT 库直连，每个假设
  都要人类代跑），实际墙钟时间超过一天；且四单合计留下一个 spec 漂移 + 一个空白档案，
  这些债的偿还成本没有计入"小时级"。
- **公平的结论**：流程外快在"省掉了评审与留痕"，而不是快在"做得更聪明"。
  hotfix 通道（§六-4）的设计目标就是把这两件事解耦——留痕的最小成本压到几分钟，
  让"快"不再必须以"漂移"为代价。

### 质量·速度·准确率：能算的数字与算不了的原因

把散在各处的数据收拢成三组指标。**先声明最大的方法论局限：没有对照组**——
同团队同项目不用 apriori 的数据不存在，下面所有数字都只能纵向比（流程内 vs 流程外、
轮次之间），不能横向断言"比不用快/好 X%"。

**① 代码质量（间接指标）**

| 指标 | 值 | 读法 |
|---|---|---|
| 测试:生产代码比 | 1 : 5.5（1012692 实测，173 行换 957 行测试） | 偏高是 spec 驱动的直接后果：每个 scenario 必须有绑定测试 |
| 主 change 交付时验证面 | 后端 148/0、前端 vitest 64/64、E2E 64/64、绑定门 ORPHAN 0 / UNIDENTIFIED 0 | "全绿交付"是常态而非里程碑 |
| 修复回归率 | **3/24 = 12.5%**（24 条缺陷条目中 3 条由修复引入） | 这是质量最刺眼的数字；三条全部同型（改了契约只验证了现象），P8 拦下其中两例 |
| reopen 率 | 1/14 = 7%（仅 1012669 一单 reopen） | reopen 那单一次暴露 4 个独立缺陷，说明首轮修复"就单修单"没有外扩排查 |

**② 开发速度（吞吐视角，补耗时账的另一面）**

| 指标 | 值 | 读法 |
|---|---|---|
| 需求→可提测 | 主 change 8 天（5 仓、83 场景 store 的主体部分） | 无基线可比，仅作锚点 |
| 缺陷批吞吐 | 三轮提测 12 单在 **2026-08-04~08-05 两天内**全部修复归档（3 个 batch change） | 流程内的缺陷修复吞吐并不慢——"走流程=慢"在小 change 上不成立 |
| 同 tier 方差 | large change 一个 8 天、一个 4.5 小时进 STEP5 | 速度的决定变量是**人类等待的串行化程度**（关卡合并授权），不是流程本身的厚度 |
| 速度的混杂变量 | — | 执行者对代码库的熟悉度三周内单调上升、后期 change 天然更小——纵向速度提升不能全记给流程熟练 |

**③ 准确率（各角色的信噪比）**

| 角色 | 准确率数据 | 出处 |
|---|---|---|
| 执行 agent 自查 | **0/7**——七个实质问题自查一个没发现 | 1012692 |
| 异构评审 | 7/7 发现；但 23 轮 P8 里方向性发现仅 1 轮——**高召回、低单轮精度、重尾** | v51 |
| 测试方报单 | 第一轮 4.5/6 为真缺陷（75%）；UAT 轮 3/4 实为环境/数据/基准问题（25%） | 缺陷档案 |
| 执行 agent 的排查定性 | 1012739 初判"会污染归属"被自己的查证推翻；1012769 五个假设逐一被数据否定后才到真因 | 档案+会话 |

第三行值得注意：**测试方报单准确率随轮次递减**（75% → 25%），因为真缺陷被逐层修完后，
留下的"看起来不对"越来越多是环境和数据的问题。这对流程的含义是：越到后期，
**"排查定性"的占比越高、"写码修复"的占比越低**——而 apriori 的全部结构都围绕
"改代码的 change"设计，对"只排查不改码"的工作（UAT 轮的 3/4）没有任何承接形态。
这其实是 §六-4 hotfix 通道的又一个论据：它承接的不只是紧急修复，
还有"结论是不用修"的排查——后者今天连一个归档的地方都没有。

### 耗时账对 roadmap 的直接含义

1. 评审是成本主体、且重尾——**动轮次结构（软收敛、关卡合并）比动单轮成本更有效**。
2. tier 定错一档，成本差一个量级——"批次"形态（§六-12）不是体验优化，是成本问题。
3. 执行者的低级错误由轮次买单——**机械前置检查**（类名 grep、版本核对这类
   进 P7 的自检清单）每省一轮返工就是净赚 20-30 分钟。
4. 人肉数据中转是隐性大头——它不是 apriori 的问题，但 STEP1 假设登记（§六-6）
   把"要查什么"提前列成清单后，中转次数至少可以合并。

---

===== SOURCE §六 P0 =====
## 六、改进 roadmap（只谈方向与优先级，不动代码）

按"墙"组织，每条注明落点类型（CLI / runbook / 模板 / 新概念），供你排期。

### P0 —— 墙一：让 gate 在棕地可信

1. **gate 接受 id-pattern**（CLI）：从 `--id-pattern` 或 process-config 读，
   后者更优——它是项目恒量，每次手输必忘，忘了静默不匹配。
2. **区分"store 健康"与"本 change 达标"两个视角**（CLI+runbook）：
   给 verify 一个"本 change 达标"的一等公民形态（`--change` 语义收窄为
   "本 change 场景 ∪ 本 change 测试"），历史缺口进另一份报告而不是混进本 change 的 verdict。
   多 change 并行时各自独立可绿。
3. **MODIFIED 整块替换的完整性机械检查**（CLI）：现在靠 LLM 评审逐行比对兜底，
   这是把确定性问题交给注意力。

### P0 —— 墙二：change 外的最小回写单元

4. **`hotfix` 形态**（新概念+CLI）：一个 10 分钟能走完的最小 bundle——
   结论一段 + spec delta + 绑定测试（或显式 no-test 理由）+ 直接归档。
   设计要点是**门槛必须低到比"不记录"只贵几分钟**，否则照样被逃逸。
   本次 UAT 四单如果有这个通道，BR-007 的口径漂移就不会发生。
5. **KB 承接"上线后学到的业务事实"**（runbook/模板）：truth 的 Decisions 节
   目前只在 change 归档时追加；给它一个 change 外的追加路径（可以就是 hotfix 的一部分），
   让"换机会迁移主板"这类血泪知识有地方落。

### P1 —— 墙三：数据勘探进 STEP1

6. **gap report 增加"关键假设登记"节**（模板）：设计已在
   `apriori-补上测试缺位的设计方案.md` §3.2 写完整（登记假设而非数据、
   分场景验证手段、无法实测时降级为低置信度标记），此处只需采纳。
7. **码值字段的字典×实测值域交叉核对**（模板+可选工具）：本项目最大单一缺陷类别
   （7 个），且可机械化——字典侧解析枚举源码，实测侧一条 GROUP BY。

### P1 —— 墙四：对比型验证与存量同义

8. **五问进 P1/P5 清单，并加第六问"存量同义"**（runbook prompt）：
   前五问设计已完整，第六问是棕地增量（见 §四·墙四）。
   另按缺陷账补两条评审立场（都有 ≥3 单的实证）：
   - **P5 加"沉默错误"清单**：GROUP BY 输出非聚合列、取最新/取唯一的排序依据、
     跨模块复用的字典、同名类的 import——这四处的共同点是不报错、测试数据越干净越藏得住，
     只能靠评审带着立场逐个问（缺陷账里它们占设计评审格 8 条中的 5 条）。
   - **P1 要求 scenario 按"动作"拆而非按"条目"写**：三单同型的教训是
     一条 AC 含两个动作（搜到+解释、选最新+回退、保留六项）时，实现只做第一个动作
     照样让条目级验收打上勾。scenario 粒度 = 可独立验证的动作，这条值得写成书写规则。
9. **E2E 定位改写**（runbook）：明确"截图证明不了两处一致"，
   对比型用例（取 A 路径结果 + 取 B 路径结果 + 断言一致）是独立的用例形态。

### P2 —— 体验债
===== TRUTH FILES =====
--- apriori/truth/archive-merge.md
# TRUTH — archive-merge (`lib/archive-merge.js`, CLI subcommand `archive`)

## Contract (code-is-truth)

source-commit: 4127653

**Responsibility.** Merge a change's delta spec into the living store by stable Requirement ID (STEP6 archive action); move the change dir to `changes/archive/`. Zero deps. 3.1: also the home of the merge-family pure helpers shared with `verify --change` — delta discovery, projection building, CAS base stamps, realpath containment — plus the high-level `archive --change` transaction and the `stamp` subcommand.

**Delta format.** `## ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED Requirements` (h2 sections) containing `### Requirement: <name>` blocks (h3); RENAMED carries `- Old -> New` lines; optionally ONE CAS stamp line `<!-- apriori-base: sha256:<64hex> | new -->` before the first section. Parsing: `parseDeltaStrict(text)` → `{ delta, stamp, problems[] }` — since delta-consumption (3.3.x) a sequential fully-consuming line walker: every line outside opaque ``` fences belongs to exactly one legal construct (file preamble, legal section heading, section-preamble free text, requirement block, RENAMED rename line), and anything else — unrecognized h2 headings (one problem per heading, its lines attributed to NOTHING, stamps included), requirement/scenario markers outside their legal home, duplicate requirement names (within/across sections), and stamp problems (malformed/duplicate/late, matched in every non-skipped state) — is a `problems[]` entry carrying its 1-based line number; CRLF parses identically to LF; well-formed deltas parse byte-identically to the pre-walker grammar; `parseDelta(text)` = its delta only; `parseRequirementsStrict(text)` → `{ map, duplicates[] }` (first block wins, duplicates reported); `parseRequirements` = its map; `deltaOpCount(delta)` → total parsed ops (0 = no recognized section OR recognized sections with no parseable requirement/rename lines). `parseStamp(text)` → `{ stamp, problems }` — any standalone comment line ATTEMPTING apriori-base that fails the strict form is a problem, never silently 'unstamped'. `fingerprint(text)` → `sha256:<64hex>` over CRLF/CR→LF-normalized content.

**Public interface.**
- `merge(storeText, delta, change)` — PURE. → `{ store:Map, merged, modified, deprecated, renamed, conflicts, unchanged }`. Duplicate requirement names in the STORE text → conflicts (corruption guard). Order of application: RENAMED first (in-place key swap preserving document order), then ADDED, MODIFIED, REMOVED.
  - ADDED on existing name: identical trimmed content → `unchanged` (idempotent rerun no-op) UNLESS the name was created by this run's RENAMED (`renamedTargets`) → conflict.
  - MODIFIED replaces the whole block; missing target → conflict; delta block trim-equal to the store block → `unchanged` (the rerun/idempotence signature, since cas-enforcement).
  - REMOVED keeps the block, injects `_deprecated (superseded by <change>)_` into the heading line.
  - RENAMED with source gone + target present → `unchanged` ("already renamed" rerun signature); source gone + target absent → conflict; target already exists → conflict.
  - REMOVED with target gone but `<name>  _deprecated (superseded by <THIS change>)_` present → `unchanged` ("already deprecated" rerun signature); deprecated by a DIFFERENT change → conflict.
  - ANY conflict → nothing written (CLI exit 1).
- `renderStore(storeText, store)` — preserves the store's header (text before the first `### Requirement:`), then blocks joined with `\n`.
- `archiveStamp(d)` → `YYYY-MM-DDThhmm` (colon-free, CLI clock).
- `CHANGE_NAME_RE` = `/^[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*$/` (same rule as `apriori new`).
- `archiveChangeDir(changesDir, name, now, ops?)` — validates name; realpath containment on BOTH source and destination (a symlinked `archive/` pointing outside is rejected before any move); `renameSync` (via ops when given) to `changes/archive/<stamp>-<name>/`; returns dest or null when source missing; throws on invalid name/escape.
- `containsReal(root, target)` — realpath containment; not-yet-existing targets judged by nearest existing ancestor; STRICT (root itself is not inside root); fail-closed on unreadable paths.
- `discoverDeltas(changesDirAbs, name)` → `{ files:[{abs,suffix}], errors[] }` — validates name (CHANGE_NAME_RE), change-dir existence + containment, specs/ itself containment-checked before becoming a root (symlinked specs/ rejected); walk follows symlink dirents explicitly (escaping links = errors, never silent skips). Errors are VALIDATION class → exit 2 on both surfaces.
- `buildProjection(storeRoot, deltaFiles, change)` → `{ texts:Map<suffix,text>, modules, perModule, conflicts, casMismatches, hygiene, validation, unstampedMutations, notes, repaired }` — all store files pass through; each delta strict-parsed, CAS-checked (mismatch+all-ops-unchanged → repaired/noted instead of failed), merged in memory; nothing touches disk.
- `archiveChange({cwd, change, changesDir, changesDirExplicit, write, ops})` → `{ code, out[], err[] }` — four phases, NO staging phase and NO post-commit writes of any kind (change-bundle, 4.0.0): the change dir is a complete bundle (requirement/ history, review/ evidence, gap-report.md, specs/ already inside), so the single atomic move carries everything; the command reads and writes nothing under any legacy root, is track-agnostic, and never deletes `spike/` (executor protocol, pre-archive). The four phases: preflight (discovery+hygiene+CAS+merges+pre-existing-temp guard+destination containment; any failure → nothing written), stage (`<store>.tmp-archive`, this-run cleanup on failure), commit (sorted per-file atomic renames; mid-commit failure → exact committed/not-committed/temps report, NO rollback), move (only changesDirExplicit, only after all commits). `ops` ({writeFileSync,renameSync,rmSync}) is the fault-injection seam.
- `stampCli(argv)` — exactly one argv entry; absent file → `new` form; directory/unreadable → exit 2.
- `cli(argv)`: high-level form `--change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]` (no --store/--delta) → archiveChange; single-file form `--store <FILE> --delta <FILE> --change <name> [--write] [--changes-dir <dir>]` unchanged from 3.0.1 EXCEPT it now checks a present CAS stamp (mismatch → exit 1, nothing written) and rejects malformed deltas via parseDeltaStrict problems. Mixing --change with only one of --store/--delta → exit 2. Exit: 0 merged/no-op · 1 conflict/CAS/hygiene/zero-op/stage/commit/move-failure/pre-existing-temp · 2 usage/invalid name/missing change dir/containment.

**Transactional write ordering (`--write`).** store staged to `<store>.tmp-archive` → dir move (atomic rename) → store committed via `renameSync(tmp, store)`. Move failure → tmp discarded, store byte-for-byte untouched. Limits: (a) tmp+rename gives single-file replace atomicity, NOT crash durability (no fsync on file or dir) — do not claim crash-durable; (b) the final `renameSync(tmp, store)` is NOT guarded — if it throws AFTER the change dir moved, the store is unchanged but the change dir is already archived and the tmp file is left behind (the "all or nothing" claim covers move failure, not store-commit failure after the move).

**Three moments.** The store file is the shared state: init = created on first `--write`; update = full rewrite via temp+rename; cleanup = none (grows monotonically; REMOVED deprecates, never deletes).

**Pitfalls.**
- `merge` compares ADDED blocks with `.trim()` equality — whitespace-only differences count as conflicts, not no-ops.
- `parseRequirementsStrict` keeps the FIRST duplicate block (previously last-wins) — visible only on invalid input, which now conflicts anyway.
- `renderStore` drops anything BETWEEN requirement blocks that isn't part of a block (inter-block prose is not preserved — only the pre-first-block header is). Needs human confirmation whether any store relies on inter-block prose (repo's own stores do not).
- CAS enforcement is GRADED since cas-enforcement (3.3.x): an unstamped delta STILL MERGES, but mutation-carrying ones (MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED — ADDED-only exempt, the exemption is clobber-focused) warn on every surface (`unstampedWarning(suffix)`, both archive forms + verify --change stderr) and `buildProjection` returns `unstampedMutations: string[]` (suffixes) that gate C7 denies by default (--no-cas / `| cas | optional |` waive it, visibly). Stamps become mandatory in 4.0. Rerun repair: a stamped file whose base mismatches but whose EVERY op is `unchanged` (the already-applied signature; MODIFIED now reports `unchanged` on trim-equality) downgrades to a `rerun accepted` note in `notes[]`/`repaired[]` and contributes no writes — the move phase completes; a mismatch with ANY real pending op still fails the whole preflight.
- Deprecation rewrites the heading LINE (`### Requirement: Name  _deprecated (superseded by x)_`), so on re-parse the requirement's map KEY includes the marker — MODIFIED/RENAMED can no longer address the block by its original name. (REMOVED reruns by the SAME change are now a no-op signature; see merge().) `DEPRECATED_RE`/`stripDeprecatedBlocks` exported — spec-runner uses them to exclude deprecated blocks from scenario collection.
- CLI parsing via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md): --help exits 0; unknown flags/stray positionals exit 2; stampCli takes exactly one positional (a dash token is an unknown flag now, not the file).

**buildProjection additions (verify-change-scope / modified-block-integrity, 2026-08-13).** The return additionally carries `deltaOps` (per-suffix operation buckets {added, modified, renamedPairs, removed} captured at THIS parse — the one delta snapshot; change-scope provenance never re-reads files) and `modifiedBlocks` (per-suffix raw {name, oldBlock, newBlock} pairs for every MODIFIED operation; rename-then-modify baselines the COMPLETE pre-rename block captured before the key swap, with a post-rename-name fallback so repaired reruns still owe their pair).

**Modified-block integrity (modified-block-integrity, 2026-08-13).** Exports `compareModifiedBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, idOf)` — a PURE engine (zero fs, zero matcher; `idOf(title)→id|null` injected): trim-equal fast path (all-empty diff, no scan, no idOf); structure scan finds scenario delimiters on the fence-STRIPPED view with the verbatim state-A regex (`\s+` may cross newlines; inline/split headings valid; fenced lines within a cross-line delimiter span still join the body), requirement heading never compared; per-key cardinality truth table (retained/titleChanged mutually exclusive on 1:1; any side >1 → ambiguous {key, side, oldCount, newCount}, skipping every other class and body comparison); order-preserving greedy subsequence over {raw, norm} lines (match on normalized, report RAW originals; requirement-prose misses carry scenario=null). `formatIntegrityHuman(entries)` renders the shared `— MODIFIED INTEGRITY —` section (significance: any of dropped/missingLines/ambiguous/titleChanged prints the entry; fields C0/DEL→`·` then whole-line 119+ellipsis cap; empty → ''). The archive surface: `cli(argv, deps)` / `archiveChange({..., idMatcherFactory})` take an injected factory `(cwd)→matcher|{error}` (bin composes it from config.resolveIdPattern + spec-runner.makeIdMatcher — this module still never requires spec-runner); `pushIntegritySection` runs after EVERY preflight guard (temp/containment included) and before write-result lines (an output-order guarantee; archive output stays buffered); a missing/failing factory degrades to one stderr line `sanitizeMsg('warning: modified-integrity ' + reason)` with nothing else changed.

## Decisions (doc-is-truth)

- D-AM-a (active): the integrity report is INFORMATIVE by contract — it never changes a verdict, an exit code or written bytes, because a machine cannot distinguish an intended deletion from a slipped one; its job is to put the difference on the table for humans and the P8 review. Ratified in modified-block-integrity (2026-08-13).
- D-AM-b (active): the archive-side id matcher arrives by dependency injection at the bin seam — archive-merge never requires spec-runner (the one-way dependency rule outranks convenience). Ratified in modified-block-integrity (2026-08-13).

- D-AM-1 (active): same-ID conflict = stop, ledger issue, human resolves — the §4.11 serialize-per-module rule; the tool never auto-resolves.
- D-AM-2 (superseded-by: D-AM-7): archive rerun idempotency as ratified in 3.0.1 covered two signatures — identical-content ADDED, and RENAMED source-gone+target-present.
- D-AM-3 (active): a content-bearing delta parsing to zero operations errors instead of silently merging nothing.
- D-AM-4 (active): REMOVED deprecates in place (audit trail), never physically deletes.
- D-AM-5 (active): CAS base stamps are opt-in — §4.11's serialize rule tooling must not break unstamped pre-3.1 deltas. Ratified in change-projection (2026-07-10).
- D-AM-6 (active): high-level archive guarantees failure-atomicity UP TO THE COMMIT POINT only; the commit phase is per-file atomic renames with an honest report on mid-commit failure and NO rollback (a rollback that half-fails is worse); crash durability (fsync) is explicitly NOT claimed (§4.8 wording discipline). Ratified in change-projection.
- D-AM-7 (superseded-by: D-AM-8): rerun idempotency covered THREE signatures — identical-content ADDED; RENAMED source-gone+target-present; REMOVED already-deprecated-by-THIS-change. MODIFIED reruns re-applied. Supersedes D-AM-2. Ratified in change-projection.
- D-AM-8 (active): rerun idempotency covers FOUR signatures — the D-AM-7 three plus MODIFIED trim-equality → `unchanged`. This is what makes the CAS rerun repair sound: a stamped delta whose base mismatches but whose every op hits a signature is already fully applied and re-runs cleanly (repaired suffixes contribute no write jobs, so they need no temp-ownership handling). Supersedes D-AM-7. Ratified in cas-enforcement.
- D-AM-10 (active): unstamped mutation deltas are DENIED by default on both archive forms (preflight set, nothing written); waivers are visible and named (--no-cas wins over | cas | optional |). verify --change stays warn-only. Ratified in cas-mandatory (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 review P0-2).
- D-AM-9 (active): the atomic move carries the whole bundle — no staging phase, no post-commit writes; the 3.4.x requirement-staging phase (req-sweep) is deleted because the bundle already contains its history. `spike/` deletion is executor protocol pre-archive; the command never touches it. Ratified in change-bundle (2026-07-12).
--- apriori/truth/spec-runner.md
# TRUTH — spec-runner (`lib/spec-runner.js`, CLI subcommand `verify`)

## Contract (code-is-truth)

source-commit: 4127653

**Responsibility.** Bind spec scenario IDs to a TAP-emitting test run; report per-scenario red/green; act as STEP5's deterministic gate. Fail-closed: a broken or vacuous run is never GREEN. `--change <name>` verifies against the in-memory PROJECTED store and its VERDICT is CHANGE-SCOPED (verify-change-scope, 2026-08-13): exit 0/1 judges only this change's requirement blocks (provenance = the projection blocks its ADDED/MODIFIED/RENAMED produce, occurrence-level, idempotent reruns included, final-deprecated excluded; rename-then-modify carries operations ["RENAMED","MODIFIED"]); cross-boundary scoped duplicates and scoped unidentified block; failure signals stay fail-closed — non-blocking ONLY when bound to an out-of-scope projection scenario OR attributed to a SIBLING active change's cleanly-parsed delta (ADDED/MODIFIED block bodies only; three-level lstat/containment guards, safeMdWalk, anomalies grant nothing and never independently error); ID-less failures and unattributable failing orphans block. The same run carries a COMPLETE six-class store report (informative). Solely for --change, a non-zero test-process status explained by parsed failures (failCount>0) with a clean change verdict is GREEN exit 0 (scoped amendment to D-SR-x). Zero-scope truth table: removal-only / empty-block deltas are vacuous-GREEN with an explicit note; an all-empty projection stays ERROR (D-SR-1's global face). One projection, one test spawn, one TAP parse per run; sibling and old-block titles ride the ONE title batch.

**Public interface (module exports).**
- `leadId(text, idRe)` → leading ID or null. Word-boundary strict: `XX-01b`, `XX-01_x` are NOT `XX-01`.
- `stripFences(text)` → text with ``` fenced blocks removed (fenced scenarios are documentation, not spec).
- `mdFiles` is internal; `collectScenarios(specTargets, idRe)` → `{ byId, unidentified:[[file,title]], files, missingTargets, duplicates:[{id,files}] }`. A target may be a dir (recursed for `*.md`, sorted) or a single file (accepted as-is, ANY extension); dedupes overlapping targets. Scenario discovery matches exact h4 lines `#### Scenario: …` only. Scenarios inside DEPRECATED requirement blocks (heading carries `_deprecated (superseded by …)_`) are excluded in EVERY form — not demanded, not listed; their lingering tests turn ORPHAN.
- `collectScenariosFromTexts(Map<label,text>, idRe)` — same scan over in-memory texts (the projection path); labels are store-relative suffixes.
- `parseTap(out, idRe)` → `{ results: Map<id,{pass,fail,skip}>, untagged:[], untaggedFails, bailout, plans:number[], points, dupNumbers:number[] }`. Only lines shaped exactly `ok|not ok <n> - <name>` BIND — a TAP result line without the ` - ` separator produces no binding. Since tap-plan (3.3.x) a per-line pre-pass (one trailing `\r` stripped) additionally collects: `plans` = every top-level `1..N` (trailing `# SKIP`/`# TODO` directive legal, N still parses); `points` = count of top-level result tokens `/^(?:ok|not ok)(?:\s|$)/` (bare/unnumbered points count; `ok:`-prefixed and indented lines never do); `dupNumbers` = complete numeric point tokens (`ok 1abc` stays unnumbered) seen more than once, numerically. `# SKIP`/`# TODO` counts as skip (proves nothing); untagged `not ok` without directive increments `untaggedFails`; `Bail out!` line captured.
- `zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged)` → true when output exists but no TAP line parsed AND no `TAP version`/`1..N` plan line (plan line = TAP reporter with an empty suite, not a reporter problem).
- `runTestCommand(cmd, cwd)` → `{ out (stdout+\n+stderr), status (null if signal-killed), signal, error }` via `spawnSync(shell:true)`.
- `evaluate(byId, unidentified, results)` → `{ boundGreen, boundRed, unbound, orphan, unidentified, clean }`. A scenario whose only results are skips is UNBOUND.
- `verify(opts {specs:[], change, testCmd, idPattern, cwd})` — with `change`: builds the projection via archive-merge's discoverDeltas/buildProjection (roots `<cwd>/apriori/{changes,specs}`); ANY projection failure (validation/hygiene/CAS/merge-conflict) → run with `errors` set and NO test executed; success → normal pipeline over projected texts; run gains `projection: {change, modules, conflicts}`. Without `change`: unchanged. → run object `{ verdict, results, fileCount, failCount, scenarioCount, missingTargets, duplicates, bailout, noTap, exec{status,signal,error}, errors }`. `failCount` = tagged fails + `untaggedFails`.
- `infraErrors(run)` → string[] (empty = trustworthy). Triggers: missing spec target · zero spec files · zero scenarios · spawn error · signal kill · bailout · zero-TAP-parsed · plan trio (defensive defaults keep legacy run shapes valid: multiple top-level plans → error naming the cure; exactly one plan and plan ≠ points → truncated/garbled; exactly one plan and duplicate point numbers) · non-zero exit with `failCount === 0` (TAP picture doesn't explain the exit). Gate surfaces any of these as ERROR exit 2 (`verify: <msg>`), outranking a C1 binding block; doctor's probe classification intentionally does not consume infraErrors.
- `formatReport(...)` → human report; RESULT line: `ERROR — run not trustworthy (see stderr)` / `GREEN — spec is the test suite` / `GAPS`. Duplicates block GREEN.
- `verifyJson(run)` → `{ clean, result: GREEN|GAPS|ERROR, errors, specFiles, exec, duplicates, boundGreen[], boundRed[], unbound[], orphan[], unidentified[] }`; `--change` runs add `projection` (never present otherwise); GREEN/GAPS `--change` runs additionally carry `storeReport` (six classes; boundGreen a bare count; array length = count; unattributedFailures {count,lines}), `changeScope` ({requirements:[{file,name,operations[]}] sorted, scenarioIds sorted}) and `modifiedIntegrity` (one entry per MODIFIED operation, empty-diff entries included; old-block titles ride the ONE title batch as a non-binding segment) — all three ABSENT (never null) on every ERROR class and on every `--specs` run (state-A byte goldens in test/fixtures/specs-golden/ prove --specs byte-identity). Since cas-enforcement (3.3.x): `--change` runs carry `projection.unstampedMutations` (suffixes, `[]` when none) + `projection.notes`; the cli prints one stderr warning per unstamped mutation delta — verdict untouched (WARN grade).
- `configTestCmd(cwd)` → test-cmd from `apriori/process-config.md`'s `| test-cmd | … |` row, or null.
- `cli(argv)` → exit code via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md): --help exits 0; unknown flags/stray positionals exit 2. `--specs <dir...>` OR `--change <name>` (mutually exclusive) `--test-cmd "<cmd>" [--id-pattern] [--cwd] [--json]`; test-cmd falls back to config row. Exit: 0 GREEN · 1 gaps/duplicates · 2 infra/usage error, incl. all projection failure classes.
- `DEFAULT_ID` = `[A-Z]+-\d+` — DEFINED in `lib/config.js` (the zero-dep resolution home) and re-exported here unchanged. The effective id-pattern resolves flag > config `id-pattern` row > DEFAULT_ID via `config.resolveIdPattern(cwd, flagValue)` (flag by PRESENCE; empty flag = flag-origin error; invalid source = sanitized origin-naming error; a present flag never consults the config).
- `makeIdMatcher(resolved)` → `{ batch(texts) → {ids:(string|null)[]} | {failure} }` — ONE recognition contract (leadId semantics) on two channels: flag/default origins inline; CONFIG origin runs every actual application inside a terminable child (`lib/id-match-child.js`, spawned `shell:false` per frozen `CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS` {timeout:2000, killSignal:SIGKILL}, stdin `{pattern,texts}` → stdout strictly `{ids}` single-key, `ids.length === texts.length`, elements string|null). Failure classes — timeout (ETIMEDOUT/SIGKILL classified FIRST), spawn-error, signal, non-zero-exit, malformed-output — all fail closed through `matcherFailureMsg` (sanitized, names process-config). `_setChildRunner` is the test seam. Exported: `makeIdMatcher`, `_setChildRunner`, `matcherFailureMsg`, `CHILD_SPAWN_OPTS`.
- Pattern-error messages are sanitized WHOLE (`config.sanitizeMsg`: control chars → `·`, ≤200 units incl. ellipsis; engine e.message never concatenated); validation happens before any spec read or test spawn (a `--change` run's invalid-pattern early exit keeps the `projection` field via enumeration-only discovery).
- `collectScenarios`/`collectScenariosFromTexts`/`parseTap` accept an optional trailing `matcher` param (default: inline from the idRe) — collection is structure-first (scanTitles) then one batch bind; a matcher failure surfaces as `collected.failure` / `parsed.matchFailure` and the run fails closed before/after the test command respectively.

**Data flow.** spec targets → collectScenarios → run test cmd once → parseTap → evaluate → infraErrors → report/JSON. One test-command invocation per verify run; no state persists between runs (stateless, reads FS + spawns only).

**Three moments.** No external shared state. FS reads only; the spawned test command is the only side effect.

**Pitfalls.**
- `--specs` consumes following args until the next `--` flag — a path starting with `--` is impossible.
- A direct-file `--specs` target is read whatever its extension; only directory recursion filters to `.md`.
- `exec.status` is null when signal-killed; the non-zero-exit rule explicitly guards `status !== null`.
- Requires `./archive-merge` (one-way: archive-merge never requires spec-runner).

## Decisions (doc-is-truth)

- D-SR-x-scoped (active): D-SR-x is AMENDED for `--change` only — a non-zero exec status fully explained by parsed failures no longer forces a non-zero exit when the change verdict is clean (the failures stay visible in the store report); an unexplained non-zero remains ERROR 2; the `--specs` form keeps D-SR-x verbatim. Ratified in verify-change-scope (2026-08-13).
- D-SR-z (active): sibling attribution is an EXEMPTION source and therefore strict — only cleanly-parsed sibling ADDED/MODIFIED block scenarios count; every anomaly grants nothing (skipping is the fail-closed direction) and never independently creates an infra ERROR (a broken sibling must not block other changes' gates). Ratified in verify-change-scope (2026-08-13, P8-driven).

- D-SR-6b (active): the id-pattern is a PROJECT CONSTANT with source-differentiated trust — the `--id-pattern` flag is operator-interactive (compile-check only, in-process matching); a process-config row is repository input CI consumes automatically, so its every actual application runs in a SIGKILL-able child; residual risk (adversarial pattern+title pairs) accepted and documented — full mitigation would need out-of-process matching everywhere, deliberately not done. Ratified in gate-id-pattern (2026-08-13, review-driven through three design generations: blanket rejection → probe battery → terminable child).

- D-SR-1 (active): fail-closed is a hard product stance — exit taxonomy 0/1/2; a vacuous or untrustworthy run must never exit 0. Ratified with 3.0.1 (owner + external review 2026-07-10).
- D-SR-2 (active): skipped tests prove nothing — a scenario with only skips is UNBOUND, not green.
- D-SR-3 (active): duplicate scenario IDs are a binding ambiguity → GAPS (exit 1), not a warning.
- D-SR-4 (active): the binding gate speaks TAP only; non-TAP layers (e.g. Playwright E2E) sit above the gate as additional exit conditions, never replace it.
- D-SR-5 (active): deprecated requirement blocks are excluded from scenario collection in ALL verify forms — post-REMOVED stores must not demand tests for removed behavior forever; lingering tests surface as ORPHAN. Ratified in change-projection (2026-07-10, STEP0 REQ-2).
- D-SR-6 (active): mid-change, the STEP5 gate is `verify --change` (projection); plain `--specs` verify is the post-archive/store-only form. Ratified in change-projection.
- D-SR-x (active): any top-level non-directive `not ok` blocks GREEN whatever its shape — unattributed failures are GAPS (exit 1) even on exit 0, infra ERRORs keep precedence, and a non-zero exec status never exits 0. Ratified in unattributed-fail (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 review P0-1).
- D-SR-y (active): TAP is parsed by a version-aware line lexer over stdout only — closed version matrix {12,13,14,absent}, escape-aware directive splitting, opaque fail-closed YAML blocks, pragma-only ignoring; stderr is a reported diagnostics channel, never parsed. Ratified in tap-contract (2026-07-12, GPT-5.6 fourth review).
--- apriori/truth/gate.md
# TRUTH — gate (`lib/gate.js`, CLI subcommand `gate`)

## Contract (code-is-truth)

source-commit: 4127653

**Responsibility.** Aggregate the MECHANICAL exit conditions for one change into one exit code: 0 PASS · 1 BLOCKED · 2 evaluation untrustworthy. Strictly read-only. PASS covers machine checks only — human gates remain human (stated in output).

**Public interface.**
- `runGate({cwd, change, testCmd})` → `{ code, stage: 'in-flight'|'archived'|null, checks:[{id,status:'pass'|'blocked'|'n/a',detail}], result:'PASS'|'BLOCKED'|'ERROR', blocked, errors, change }`.
- `resolveChange(cwd, name)` → `{stage, dir}` or `{error}` — in-flight `apriori/changes/<name>` first (realpath containment vs changes dir); else archived: basenames matching `/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-<name>$/` under `changes/archive/` that stat as DIRECTORIES, sorted, last; containment vs the ARCHIVE root (a symlink pointing elsewhere under changes/ is an escape).
- `cli(argv)`: `--change <name> [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--id-pattern <re>] [--cwd <dir>] [--json / --no-cas (C7 waiver, visible in the check detail)]` — `--id-pattern` judged by PRESENCE (an empty flag is a flag-origin error, never a config fallback) via the shared strict parser (truth/args.md); testCmd falls back to `configTestCmd(cwd)`; neither → exit 2. `--json` = pure JSON in EVERY outcome class incl. parse errors (the jsonError seam).

**The seven checks.** C1 binding (in-flight → `verify({change, idPattern})` projected; archived → `verify({specs:[apriori/specs], idPattern})`; the effective id-pattern = flag > process-config `id-pattern` row > DEFAULT_ID, resolved inside verify; invalid pattern → gate ERROR exit 2 through the existing errors path; verify infra errors → gate exit 2). The in-flight C1 consumes the CHANGE-SCOPED verdict (detail `verify GREEN (in-flight, change-scoped)` / `verify GAPS: <scoped counts>`, each with the six-count store summary suffix `; store: R red, U unbound, O orphan, N unidentified, A unattributed, D duplicate(s) outstanding`) — parallel changes' gates go green independently; the archived stage still verifies the whole store · C2 tasks.md zero `- [ ]` (`[x]`/`[X]` checked; absent → n/a on trivial, blocked otherwise) · C3 flow-state legality (required keys change/tier/track/lineage/current-step; placeholder = contains `<`/`>`; exact step/tier enums; change must equal --change; file missing → exit 2) · C4 ledger at `<dir>/review/issues.md` (the resolved change dir's bundle) BOTH stages, speaking the terminal-state vocabulary since ledger-states (3.3.x): statuses classified by `classifyStatus` (exported; leading token case-insensitive, `rejected-verified` alternated before `rejected`) — unknown statuses block at both stages; `open` blocks; `rejected`/`rejected-verified`/`waived` need a word character after the token; a `waived` row needs same-entry human evidence in the change's flow-state `gates:` block (one entry carrying the row ID as an exact token — LS-1 never matches inside LS-10 — plus /waiv/i); at the ARCHIVED stage every row must be terminal (`verified`/`rejected-verified`/`waived`/`advisory-acked`) — `fixed` and plain `rejected` block with reviewer-verify/concur-or-human-waive cures; in-flight `fixed` and reasoned `rejected` pass (absent ledger → n/a on trivial) · C5 verdict evidence (docs `<dir>/review/*.md`, minus `issues.md` and raw stems, regular files only — symlinked doc blocks; `/^VERDICT:/m` doc needs a REGULAR (lstat-first) `<dir>/review/<stem>-raw.*` beside it). Shared C4/C5 evidence-root guard `reviewDirDefect(dir)`: `<dir>/review` is lstat-ed FIRST (only ENOENT is absence — a dangling symlink is a defect); symlink / non-dir / escape (containsReal) blocks BOTH C4 and C5 naming the defect · C6 KB freshness (modules = first segments of delta-spec suffixes; precondition ladder → n/a with reason; `git -C <cwd> log --oneline <stamp>..HEAD -- lib/<m>.js`: clean pass / commits block / non-zero git exit n/a). · C7 CAS default-deny (in-flight: the projection's `unstampedMutations` block naming each suffix + the stamp cure; waivers are VISIBLE — `--no-cas` flag or a `| cas | optional |` process-config row (flag wins, `configCas` leading value token case-insensitive); archived → n/a, deltas already merged; neither waiver touches verify/archive behavior)

**Pitfalls.**
- C1 runs the project's full test command — gate's runtime is dominated by it.
- C6's module→code mapping is the `lib/<m>.js` convention; anything off-convention degrades to n/a (never a false block).
- GT-12's read-only test fingerprints path+size+mtime — same-size rewrites with restored mtime would evade it; static inspection is the actual guarantee (gate performs zero writes).
- Requires `./status`, `./spec-runner`, `./archive-merge`; nothing requires gate (leaf module).

## Decisions (doc-is-truth)

- D-GT-5 (active): C1 consumes the project id-pattern (flag > config > default) through verify's shared resolution — gate never resolves or compiles patterns itself; the flag is presence-judged and an invalid effective pattern is a structured ERROR, never a silent default. Ratified in gate-id-pattern (2026-08-13).

- D-GT-1 (active): gate judges the MECHANICAL face only; its PASS never substitutes for protected human gates — the output must say so. Ratified in gate-command (2026-07-11).
- D-GT-2 (active): infra failures (git absent, bad stamp commit, unmappable module) yield n/a with a reason, never a fabricated block — false blocks train humans to ignore the gate.
- D-GT-3 (amended by change-bundle, 2026-07-12): the raw-evidence naming rule is normative: a review doc with stem S archives its raw as `<dir>/review/S-raw.*` inside its change bundle; pre-4.0 archives were migrated to this shape.
- D-GT-4 (active): C4/C5 read the bundle evidence root `<dir>/review/` and work identically in-flight and archived because the evidence travels with the dir; the root itself is guarded lstat-first — dangling symlinks are defects, never absence. Ratified in change-bundle (2026-07-12).

printf '%s\\n' '===== RUNBOOK CN STRUCTURE ====='
